# Commit evidence — 2026-07-22: display gates, committed bend store, gallery round

Branch `next`. One batch: the display-gate architecture (producer→consumer
bend store + gated regeneration), the gallery reorganization round, and regen
fixes. No Lean; solver untouched (atlas consumes its committed output).

## Staged tree

**builders/personal.py** — (a) Committed bend store: producer persists solved
bends to `data/bends/<nid>.json` — ONLY as complete, gate-passing snapshots;
consumer builds from the store, and with a store present the solver is NEVER
invoked (store-only rule; incomplete store ⇒ morphs stay unbuilt — the
no-giant-chase ruling in code). (b) Always-regenerate: every displayed
artifact is rebuilt from committed bends through the gates each build; no
reuse (regeneration measured byte-deterministic), stale morph GLBs deleted
unconditionally. (c) Length-fidelity gate (PD spec: every displayed edge
within 1% of exact): normalized Lorentz metric post-`center` with per-point
finite/timelike/future validation; per-event normalized Lorentz closure on
every development closure event (≤1% of ell); Euclidean closure over all
directed face adjacencies (`euclid_closure_resid`) + edge lengths; develop
blow-up = hard reject. (d) Undented display gate: `dent_index` link-turning
port on displayed coordinates; coplanar-degenerate vertices skipped only
under the flat-bends certificate (missing bend ⇒ uncertified ⇒ reject).
(e) Hero tripwires raise → BUILD-FAIL; `main()` exits nonzero on any failed
net. (f) Backstop wrapper; `ATLAS_MORPHS=0` opt-out ships nothing;
`morph_note` audit field.

**builders/realize_h.py** — `develop_h` additionally returns closure events
(callers use `[0]`; the gate consumes the third element).

**builders/views.py** — morph block renders only when BOTH morph artifacts
are present.

**builders/special.py** — galleries: classics → Platonic+Archimedean only;
Odds-and-ends off + delinked; Non-prime chip after the three Primes; front
page promises morphs only where certified; dented gallery self-regenerates
from committed `data/walks` via `build.model_glb` (∅-first dent sets, orbit
×k + gap), geometry-exact with published bytes; CSS extended.

**regen_personal.sh** — `set -euo pipefail` (a failed builder step ABORTS —
the personal.py nonzero exit must stop the pipeline, per retro-G2);
numpy/scipy preflight BEFORE the destructive `rm` (wrong interpreter fails
with the site intact); dead network step `fetch_dents.py` removed (consumed
by nothing; must not be an abort vector under -e); `python3.13` + `NEO` →
sibling `projects/neo`; `NEO_SRC`/`PYTHON` overridable. Integration tests
executed: `PYTHON=false` → exit 1 at preflight, site untouched (2245 nets
before and after); `set -euo pipefail; false; echo UNREACHABLE` → exit 1,
unreachable not printed. DISCLOSED: python3.13 lacks scipy on this box, so
the full-regen path (classify.py, its only importer) needs
`PYTHON=/usr/bin/python3` (has numpy 2.0.2 + scipy 1.13.1) or a scipy
install for 3.13 — the consumer build path (personal/symmetry/special)
needs only numpy and runs under python3.13.

**data/bends/** (2245 json, 56 MB) — the committed bend store (2026-07-22
full solver run; deterministic — bend-identical repeat solves, byte-identical
GLB round-trip).

**data/dentings.tsv** — published dent-set scan manifest (47 nets, 195
dentings).

**notes/gate-calibration-20260722/** — the calibration record: `rel_v50.tsv`,
`klein_v50.tsv` (superseded metrics, kept as history), `edge_len.tsv`,
`dent_turn.tsv`, `numcal.txt` (|num| separation ×1.1e5; 4449 skips, 0
uncertified), `closure_C.txt` (measured closure-noise coefficient),
`final_sweep.txt` (zero-state).

**MORPH_GATE_TODO.md** — the full design record: spec, superseded metrics,
all seven G1 rounds with findings and fixes, calibrations, production catches, and the PD certify-where-resolvable ruling.

**notes/codex-consults/** — the 2026-07-21 G2 transcript + seven G1 consults
(161343, 162535, 162856, 163649, 164416, 164845, 175729).

**.claude/launch.json** — local preview server config.

fixes. No Lean; solver untouched (atlas consumes its committed output).

## Staged tree

**builders/personal.py** — (a) Committed bend store: producer persists solved
bends to `data/bends/<nid>.json` — ONLY as complete, gate-passing snapshots;
consumer builds from the store, and with a store present the solver is NEVER
invoked (store-only rule; incomplete store ⇒ morphs stay unbuilt — the
no-giant-chase ruling in code). (b) Always-regenerate: every displayed
artifact is rebuilt from committed bends through the gates each build; no
reuse (regeneration measured byte-deterministic), stale morph GLBs deleted
unconditionally. (c) Length-fidelity gate (PD spec: every displayed edge
within 1% of exact): normalized Lorentz metric post-`center` with per-point
finite/timelike/future validation; per-event normalized Lorentz closure on
every development closure event (≤1% of ell); Euclidean closure over all
directed face adjacencies (`euclid_closure_resid`) + edge lengths; develop
blow-up = hard reject. (d) Undented display gate: `dent_index` link-turning
port on displayed coordinates; coplanar-degenerate vertices skipped only
under the flat-bends certificate (missing bend ⇒ uncertified ⇒ reject).
(e) Hero tripwires raise → BUILD-FAIL; `main()` exits nonzero on any failed
net. (f) Backstop wrapper; `ATLAS_MORPHS=0` opt-out ships nothing;
`morph_note` audit field.

**builders/realize_h.py** — `develop_h` additionally returns closure events
(callers use `[0]`; the gate consumes the third element).

**builders/views.py** — morph block renders only when BOTH morph artifacts
are present.

**builders/special.py** — galleries: classics → Platonic+Archimedean only;
Odds-and-ends off + delinked; Non-prime chip after the three Primes; front
page promises morphs only where certified; dented gallery self-regenerates
from committed `data/walks` via `build.model_glb` (∅-first dent sets, orbit
×k + gap), geometry-exact with published bytes; CSS extended.

**regen_personal.sh** — `set -euo pipefail` (a failed builder step ABORTS —
the personal.py nonzero exit must stop the pipeline, per retro-G2);
numpy/scipy preflight BEFORE the destructive `rm` (wrong interpreter fails
with the site intact); dead network step `fetch_dents.py` removed (consumed
by nothing; must not be an abort vector under -e); `python3.13` + `NEO` →
sibling `projects/neo`; `NEO_SRC`/`PYTHON` overridable. Integration tests
executed: `PYTHON=false` → exit 1 at preflight, site untouched (2245 nets
before and after); `set -euo pipefail; false; echo UNREACHABLE` → exit 1,
unreachable not printed. DISCLOSED: python3.13 lacks scipy on this box, so
the full-regen path (classify.py, its only importer) needs
`PYTHON=/usr/bin/python3` (has numpy 2.0.2 + scipy 1.13.1) or a scipy
install for 3.13 — the consumer build path (personal/symmetry/special)
needs only numpy and runs under python3.13.

**data/bends/** (2245 json, 56 MB) — the committed bend store (2026-07-22
full solver run; deterministic — bend-identical repeat solves, byte-identical
GLB round-trip).

**data/dentings.tsv** — published dent-set scan manifest (47 nets, 195
dentings).

**notes/gate-calibration-20260722/** — the calibration record: `rel_v50.tsv`,
`klein_v50.tsv` (superseded metrics, kept as history), `edge_len.tsv`,
`dent_turn.tsv`, `numcal.txt` (|num| separation ×1.1e5; 4449 skips, 0
uncertified), `closure_C.txt` (measured closure-noise coefficient),
`final_sweep.txt` (zero-state).

**MORPH_GATE_TODO.md** — the full design record: spec, superseded metrics,
all seven G1 rounds with findings and fixes, calibrations, production catches, and the PD certify-where-resolvable ruling.

**notes/codex-consults/** — the 2026-07-21 G2 transcript + seven G1 consults
(161343, 162535, 162856, 163649, 164416, 164845, 175729).

**.claude/launch.json** — local preview server config.

  (`closure 5.58e-01, eerr 1.11e-16` — the closure-vacuousness proof);
  bad input through real `main()` → exit 1; healthy → exit 0;
  `morphs=False` → no GLBs, no flags, store untouched.

  off-hyperboloid, unnormalized metric read 7.3e-5, normalized 5.5e-2) and
  v28 (per-event closure 1.49e-2, invisible to retained-edge metric) — both
  rebuilt, morphs omitted with measured reasons. v>50 notes made truthful
  (55 incomplete-store / 66 gate-failed).
- Site state: 2245 pages rendered; galleries + front page regenerated;
  dented gallery 242/242 filenames match published, geometry-exact.

## Retroactive G2 (the automatic gate failed open)

The commit-time gate errored (codex context exhausted on the raw 2245-file
diff; `tokens used 0`, exit 1) and the wrapper FAILED OPEN — the initial
commit of this tree landed unreviewed. A retroactive review of the exact
tree (full code diff + summarized data files) returned BLOCK
(notes/codex-consults/2026-07-22-180254-g2retro-1c4fcf6.txt) with three
findings, all fixed in the amended commit: (1) regen_personal.sh masked
the failure signal (`set -u` only) → `set -euo pipefail` + preflight +
executed propagation tests; (2) the evidence lacked an entry-point
integration test → executed (see regen_personal.sh above; the preflight
also EXPOSED the python3.13 scipy gap, disclosed); (3) the commit message
overclaimed "every displayed artifact regenerates" → corrected to the
gated GLB artifacts (ideal_net/clers_layout SVGs remain existence-cached,
ungated and deterministic). Gate-hook fail-open flagged to Peter as a
process bug (fail closed on codex error; summarize oversized diffs).

## New conventions / invariants (G1)

Two display invariants (1% length fidelity incl. closure; dent_index with
certified coplanar skip) — spec and full revision history in
MORPH_GATE_TODO.md; calibration evidence in notes/gate-calibration-20260722/.

G1 consults (all in notes/codex-consults/, same-day, seven rounds):
- r1 161343: REVISE (6 findings) — fixed (normalized metric, post-center
  certification, certified coplanar skip, isfinite, reuse recert,
  exit chain).
- r2 162535: REVISE (4 residuals) — fixed (missing-bend uncertified,
  storeless reuse removed, store-only rule, hero recertification).
- r3 162856: REVISE (2 residuals) — fixed (reuse ELIMINATED:
  always-regenerate; euclid+hyperbolic closure gates added after the
  corrupted-store test exposed closure vacuousness — rigid placement
  keeps kept edges exact, walklib.develop computes no residual).
- r4 163649: REVISE (3 findings) — fixed (opt-out hygiene,
  complete-snapshot-only store persistence, per-event Lorentz closure).
- r5 164416: REVISE (2 findings) — fixed (stale-morph deletion FIRST —
  proven by pre-planted-stale + failing-build test; materiality made
  scale-aware after a fixed 1e-9 was measured to false-trip every
  deepest rung via eps*s^2 Minkowski cancellation).
- r6 164845: REVISE (clamp inverted to acceptance hole) — fixed by the
  conservative-upper-bound rule; the noise coefficient was then MEASURED
  (closure_C.txt: 756001 events, large-s bound 62; C_CLOSURE=128); the
  25 small nets unresolvable at 1% in float64 went to Peter, whose
  recorded RULING is certify-where-resolvable: closure certified
  wherever float64 resolves 1%, unverifiable events skipped + counted +
  disclosed via morph_caveat, corruption rejection retained. (Incident,
  recorded in the baton: a scripted bulk edit duplicated a code block;
  caught by the spot suite, copies diffed identical, excised
  line-precise, all signatures re-verified.)
- r7 175729: APPROVE — "the implementation matches the recorded
  certify-where-resolvable semantics"; C_CLOSURE confirmed against the
  measurement; acceptance hole confirmed removed.

over 2220 nets, 0 hero failures; morph-carrying nets failing: 0. The

+# Morph gate — RESOLVED 2026-07-22 (supersedes the 2026-07-21 baton)
+
+Original plan was (1) a develop_h residual gate and (2) removing morphs
+for V > 50. Peter's rulings during implementation replaced both:
+
+- No arbitrary v-cap. Pages render morphs iff the record carries them,
+  and morphs enter the record only through the certificate. A giant that
+  certifies keeps its morphs; a small net that fails loses them.
+- Final gate spec (PD, 2026-07-22): every edge of a displayed frame has
+  the length it is supposed to have, within 1 percent.
+  Hyperbolic rungs: max over edges of |d_H(u,v) - ell(alpha)| / ell
+  <= 1e-2 (each face is an equilateral triangle of side ell(alpha), so
+  ground truth is exact and intrinsic). Euclidean end frame:
+  max |len - 1| <= 1e-2. A develop_h numerical blow-up (off-hyperboloid
+  sqrt) is a hard rejection, not a crash. Implemented in
+  builders/personal.py (EDGE_LEN_TOL, develop_h_certified, glb_movies);
+  one failing frame => NO morph GLBs, reason recorded in net.json
+  (morph_note). views.py renders the morph block only when the record
+  has the artifacts.
+- Superseded along the way (measured, then discarded): a relative
+  4-vector closure gate at 1e-6 (mis-calibrated: falsely killed ~572
+  good v<=50 morphs) and a Klein-length closure-witness gate at 1e-3.
+
+Calibration of the final gate, measured from the committed bends
+(notes/gate-calibration-20260722/edge_len.tsv, 2026-07-22): max rel edge error v<=30 = 1.11e-4;
+Euclidean heroes exact to 7.7e-14 over 2220 nets; exactly one v<=50 net
+fails (the v48, edge error ~3e-2 class at deep alpha); diverged giants
+score 0.7-1.0. Verdict deltas vs the deployed morph inventory: zero in
+both directions -- the gate confirms the site as built.
+
+Large-v morphs: no further chase (PD: "nobody will want morphs for
+large v anyway"). The ~121 v>50 nets stay morph-omitted with their
+recorded reasons; the deep-alpha store rungs for 41-58 giants remain
+unsolved and that is accepted. No doob batch.
+
+Related architecture landed with this (see data/bends/): the committed
+bend store -- personal.py persists solver output (producer) and builds
+from committed bends when present (consumer, no solver / neo / network).
+
+## Undented display gate — WIRED 2026-07-22 (PD: "fix the gate")
+
+dent_index link-turning check (port of neo/_trash-20260715/undented/
+undented/src/dent_check.c inner loop -- turning per vertex, NEVER volume
+sign) now runs on the displayed coordinates in builders/personal.py:
+- every morph rung (Klein frame post-center, exactly what renders) and
+  the Euclidean end frame, in glb_movies -- failure => morphs omitted,
+  reason in morph_note ("dented frame at alpha=...");
+- both hero paths (glb_euclid, glb_hero) -- failure raises, surfacing as
+  BUILD-FAIL: a dented hero can never ship silently.
+
+Degenerate-case handling, forced by a measured failure mode (traced on
+the v12 pancake): an exactly-coplanar vertex link makes every triple
+product B.(AxC) exact-zero; where den < 0, atan2 sits on the branch cut
+and returns +-pi by the sign of 1e-17 noise, so per-vertex totals
+{-2pi, 0, +2pi} are branch picks, not geometry. Vertices with all
+|B.(AxC)| < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL (1e-12) are therefore skipped -- a dent
+requires non-planarity; flat is the degenerate boundary, already
+certified by the solved bends (all-plus turning system). Gate constants:
+DENT_TURN_TOL = 1e-9, COPLANAR_NUM_TOL = 1e-12. Calibration over all
+2245 stores (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/dent_turn.tsv + the v10-vertex trace): zero
+displayed heroes or morph frames fail; tightest genuine margin +3.0e-7;
+noise floor ~1e-16. Spot-verified through build_net: pancake v12 (all
+vertices skipped, passes), v13, deg-7 j45 (Klein hero + 11 rungs), and
+the v48 (correctly rejected upstream by the edge-length gate, 5.9e-2).
+
+G1 round 1 (notes/codex-consults/2026-07-22-161343-g1-display-gates.txt):
+REVISE, with the formula/ring-orientation/frame choices CONFIRMED and
+six findings, all addressed in the same session:
+1. Normalized metric: d_H = acosh(-mdot(u,v)/sqrt((-mdot u)(-mdot v)));
+   raw acosh(-mdot) mis-measures off-hyperboloid drift (codex 26 percent
+   counterexample). Every point must be finite, timelike, future
+   (x4 > 0). DONE (develop_h_certified).
+2. Certify what renders: certification now runs AFTER center(); the
+   Klein projection is taken from the certified centered points. DONE.
+3. Coplanar skip could mask a near-flat dent (codex synthetic
+   counterexample at max|num|=3.4e-15): a skip now REQUIRES the
+   flat-bends certificate -- every incident bend within FLAT_BEND_TOL
+   (1e-6) of 0 or +-pi, the doubled-flat signature; coplanar without the
+   certificate rejects the frame ("uncertified degenerate"). DONE.
+   COPLANAR_NUM_TOL calibration vs smallest nondegenerate |num|:
+   measured over all stores (see numcal below).
+4. NaN pass-through: explicit isfinite rejection in both gates. DONE.
+5. Stale-artifact bypass: reuse of existing morph GLBs now goes through
+   a certification-only pass from the committed bends (glb_movies
+   write=False); failure deletes the GLBs and records the reason.
+   Partial artifacts rebuild (both-or-neither), and views.py renders the
+   morph block only when BOTH artifacts are present. DONE.
+6. Dented-hero exceptions surface as BUILD-FAIL and main() now exits
+   nonzero when any net failed. DONE.
+Declared out of scope (per consult): global self-intersection of
+displayed frames and corruption introduced downstream of certification
+(subdivision, Klein->Poincare conversion, GLB encoding) -- the gates
+certify the vertex geometry that feeds rendering, not the render
+pipeline itself.
+
+Spot-verified after revision: v12 pancake / v13 / deg-7 j45 all
+"reused (recertified)" through the store; v48 still omitted (5.93e-02
+under the normalized metric -- verdict unchanged by normalization).
+
+Revision calibration (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/numcal.txt, all
+2245 stores under the revised gates): smallest NONDEGENERATE
+max|B.(AxC)| = 1.13e-7 -- COPLANAR_NUM_TOL=1e-12 sits x1.1e5 below it
+(and the measured degenerate noise ~1e-16 x1e4 below the tolerance);
+4449 skipped vertices on 117 nets, EVERY one carrying the flat-bends
+certificate (0 uncertified). Verdicts vs built site: no morph-carrying
+net fails; the apparent deltas are v>50 giants omitted with
+incomplete stores whose STORED rungs all pass -- undecided, not
+mis-gated; their morph_note now records "store incomplete beyond
+alpha=X (deep rungs unsolved; no giant chase, PD 2026-07-22)" instead
+of the superseded 1e-6-metric reason (measured split after the note pass: 55 incomplete-store, 66
+giants failing a stored rung under the final gates, each carrying its
+real reason).
+G1 round 2 (notes/codex-consults/2026-07-22-162535-g1r2-display-gates.txt):
+REVISE -- findings 1/2/4 confirmed resolved, partial-artifact
+fix confirmed; four residuals, all fixed in-session:
+- missing bend key treated as flat -> now uncertified (bends.get(e) is
+  None => reject);
+- storeless GLB reuse rendered "uncertified" -> branch removed; such
+  GLBs are deleted and rebuilt through the gates in producer mode;
+- write=False could fall back to the solver on incomplete stores -> the
+  store-only rule: with a store present glb_movies NEVER solves, in any
+  mode; a missing rung => "store incomplete", morphs stay unbuilt (this
+  also hard-codes the no-giant-chase ruling into the builder);
+- reused rb.glb/clers.glb heroes bypassed the hero gates -> reused
+  heroes are recertified from the committed bends (edge + dent; failure
+  raises => BUILD-FAIL).
+Spot-verified: v12/v13/v34 "reused (recertified)"; v78 incomplete-store
+giant resolves "store incomplete at alpha=21.87" in 0.1s with zero
+solver calls, hero recertified in the same pass.
+G1 round 3 (notes/codex-consults/2026-07-22-162856-g1r3-display-gates.txt):
+REVISE -- 3'/5a'/5b' confirmed fixed; remaining: certifying a
+reconstruction while REUSING on-disk GLBs proves nothing about the bytes
+served, and finding 6 wanted an executed failing-exit test. Resolution:
+- Reuse ELIMINATED (G1 r3): build_net always regenerates every displayed
+  artifact from the committed bends through the gates; regeneration is
+  deterministic (measured byte-stable), so the artifact/model gap
+  vanishes by construction. Storeless nets take the producer path
+  (solve + persist) -- nothing ever renders uncertified.
+- Deg-7 fresh heroes now edge-gated too (develop_h_certified before
+  glb_hero; previously only dent-gated).
+- Exit-chain tests EXECUTED: (t2) deliberate bad input through real
+  main(): exit 1; healthy input: exit 0. (t1) corrupted-store test
+  (hero bend +0.7) EXPOSED A GATE HOLE all three consult rounds missed:
+  the corrupted hero BUILT CLEANLY -- rigid placement keeps kept edge
+  lengths unit BY CONSTRUCTION (measured eerr 1.1e-16 on the corrupt
+  net), so bad bends surface only in CLOSURE, and walklib.develop
+  computes no closure residual at all (revisited faces skipped
+  uncompared). Fixed: euclid_closure_resid() over ALL directed face
+  adjacencies gates heroes and morph euclid-ends (closure <= 1e-2, edge
+  scale 1); hyperbolic side gains the supplementary closure gate
+  (develop_h resid / raw scale <= 1e-2; calibrated: v<=30 max relative

+def euclid_closure_resid(faces, bd, pos):
+    """Max mismatch between every face's apex as re-placed from its
+    neighbor (walklib.develop's placement formula) and the kept position.
+    Rigid placement makes KEPT edge lengths unit by construction, so bad
+    bends surface only here (proven by the corrupted-store test, G1 r3):
+    walklib.develop skips revisited faces without comparing, computing no
+    residual at all. Checked over ALL directed adjacencies, a superset of
+    any single BFS's closure events. Edge scale is 1, so this bounds the
+    true edge-length error of the displayed configuration."""
+    apex = {}
+    for a, b, c in faces:
+        apex[(a, b)] = c; apex[(b, c)] = a; apex[(c, a)] = b
+    r = 0.0
+    for f0 in faces:
+        for i in range(3):
+            x, y = f0[i], f0[(i + 1) % 3]
+            d = apex[(y, x)]
+            A, B, P = pos[x], pos[y], pos[f0[(i + 2) % 3]]
+            M = 0.5 * (A + B)
+            w = P - M
+            w = w / np.linalg.norm(w)
+            n = np.cross(w, B - A)
+            n = n / np.linalg.norm(n)
+            th = bd[tuple(sorted((x, y)))]
+            D = M + (math.sqrt(3) / 2) * (-math.cos(th) * w + math.sin(th) * n)
+            r = max(r, float(np.max(np.abs(D - pos[d]))))
+    return r
+

 def glb_euclid(name, faces, V, bends, outdir):
-    pos, _ = develop(faces, {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends.items()})
+    bd = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends.items()}
+    pos, _ = develop(faces, bd)
+    # hero tripwires: closure AND edge lengths AND undented display
+    cres = euclid_closure_resid(faces, bd, pos)
+    eerr = max(abs(float(np.linalg.norm(pos[u] - pos[v])) - 1.0)
+               for u, v in _edges_of(faces))
+    mt, dbad = display_min_turn(pos, link_rings(faces), bd)
+    if not (math.isfinite(eerr) and math.isfinite(cres)) \
+            or cres > EDGE_LEN_TOL or eerr > EDGE_LEN_TOL or dbad \
+            or mt < -DENT_TURN_TOL:
+        raise RuntimeError(
+            f"hero fails display gates (closure {cres:.2e}, "
+            f"eerr {eerr:.2e}, min turn {mt:.2e})")

+# Length-fidelity gate (PD spec 2026-07-22, final): every edge of a
+# displayed frame must have the length it is supposed to have, within 1%.
+# Hyperbolic frames: every edge of every equilateral face has exact
+# hyperbolic length ell(alpha) (realize_h.side_length); the metric is
+# max over edges of |d_H(u,v) - ell| / ell, with d_H = acosh(-mdot).
+# Euclidean end frame: every edge has length 1; max |len - 1|.
+# Accumulated development divergence lands exactly here: edges whose
+# endpoints arrive via different development paths inherit the drift.
+EDGE_LEN_TOL = 1e-2
+
+
+def _edges_of(faces):
+    return {tuple(sorted((a, b))) for f in faces
+            for a, b in zip(f, f[1:] + f[:1])}
+
+
+# Undented display gate (PD 2026-07-22: "make sure what we display is
+# actually undented"). dent_index link-turning check (port of
+# undented/src/dent_check.c inner loop -- turning per vertex, NEVER
+# volume sign) run on the displayed coordinates themselves. Degenerate
+# case, measured 2026-07-22 on the v12 pancake: an exactly-coplanar
+# vertex link makes every triple product B.(AxC) exact-zero, and where
+# den < 0 the atan2 lands on the branch cut -- +-pi decided by 1e-17
+# rounding noise, so per-vertex totals {-2pi, 0, +2pi} are branch picks,
+# not geometry. Such vertices are excluded: a dent needs non-planarity,
+# and a coplanar link is the flat degenerate boundary (pancakes). Their
+# flatness is already certified by the solved bends (all-plus turning
+# system). Calibration over all 2245 stores
+# (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/dent_turn.tsv): every displayed hero
+# and morph frame passes; tightest genuine margin +3.0e-7; noise floor
+# ~1e-16.
+DENT_TURN_TOL = 1e-9        # reject a displayed frame if min turn < -this
+COPLANAR_NUM_TOL = 1e-12    # all |B.(AxC)| below this => degenerate vertex
+FLAT_BEND_TOL = 1e-6        # bend within this of {0, +-pi} counts as flat/fold
+
+
+def link_rings(faces):
+    """vertex -> cyclic neighbor ring, from the face structure."""
+    succ = {}
+    for a, b, c in faces:
+        succ.setdefault(a, {})[b] = c
+        succ.setdefault(b, {})[c] = a
+        succ.setdefault(c, {})[a] = b
+    rings = {}
+    for v, nxt in succ.items():
+        start = next(iter(nxt))
+        ring, cur = [start], nxt[start]
+        while cur != start:
+            ring.append(cur)
+            cur = nxt[cur]
+        rings[v] = ring
+    return rings
+
+
+def display_min_turn(pos, rings, bends=None):
+    """(min_turn, bad) over the displayed positions. min_turn is the
+    minimum link turning over non-degenerate vertices (+inf if none).
+
+    G1 revision (consult 2026-07-22-161343): a coplanar-degenerate vertex
+    (all |B.(AxC)| < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL, where the atan2 branch is noise)
+    is SKIPPED only when its flatness is independently certified by the
+    solved bends -- every incident bend within FLAT_BEND_TOL of 0 or
+    +-pi, the doubled-flat/pancake signature. A coplanar link WITHOUT
+    that certificate may hide a near-flat dent, so bad=True and the
+    caller must reject the frame. Nonfinite coordinates or turning sums
+    also set bad=True.
+    """
+    mt, bad = float('inf'), False
+    for v, ring in rings.items():
+        k = len(ring)
+        if k < 3:
+            continue
+        dirs = []
+        for nb in ring:
+            d = np.asarray(pos[nb], float) - np.asarray(pos[v], float)
+            n = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
+            if not math.isfinite(n):
+                bad = True
+                break
+            dirs.append(d / (n if n > 1e-15 else 1e-15))
+        else:
+            nums, dens = [], []
+            for i in range(k):
+                A, B, C = dirs[(i - 1) % k], dirs[i], dirs[(i + 1) % k]
+                nums.append(float(np.dot(B, np.cross(A, C))))
+                dens.append(float(np.dot(A, B) * np.dot(B, C) - np.dot(A, C)))
+            if max(abs(x) for x in nums) < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL:
+                flat_ok = bends is not None
+                if flat_ok:
+                    for nb in ring:
+                        b = bends.get(tuple(sorted((v, nb))))
+                        # a MISSING bend is uncertified, never flat (G1 r2)
+                        if b is None or not (abs(b) < FLAT_BEND_TOL
+                                             or abs(abs(b) - math.pi) < FLAT_BEND_TOL):
+                            flat_ok = False
+                            break
+                if not flat_ok:
+                    bad = True       # degenerate link, flatness uncertified
+                continue
+            t = sum(math.atan2(n_, d_) for n_, d_ in zip(nums, dens))
+            if not math.isfinite(t):
+                bad = True
+                continue
+            mt = min(mt, t)
+            continue
+        break                        # nonfinite direction: stop, frame bad
+    return mt, bad
+
+
+# closure-noise coefficient, MEASURED (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/
+# closure_C.txt: 756001 clean-frame events; |q-1|/(eps*s^2) median 0.38,
+# p99 7.6, large-s (>1e5) bound 62). C = 128 = 2x the measured bound.
+C_CLOSURE = 128.0
+
+
+def develop_h_certified(faces, bd, alpha_rad, edges=None):
+    """develop_h -> center -> edge-length gate ON THE CENTERED (displayed)
+    coordinates. Returns (pos_centered, err, ok, n_unverifiable):
+    err = max relative hyperbolic edge-length error vs ell(alpha);
+    n_unverifiable = closure events where the float64 noise floor alone
+    exceeds the 1% budget, so closure is certified-where-resolvable and
+    skipped there (PD ruling 2026-07-22), corruption rejection still
+    applying. Callers record a caveat when n_unverifiable > 0.
+
+    G1 revisions (consult 2026-07-22-161343): the distance is the
+    NORMALIZED form d_H = acosh(-mdot(u,v)/sqrt((-mdot(u,u))(-mdot(v,v))))
+    -- the raw acosh(-mdot) is valid only on exact unit timelike vectors
+    and silently mis-measures off-hyperboloid drift; every point must be
+    finite, timelike (-mdot(x,x) > 0), and future (x4 > 0); certification
+    runs after center() so it validates exactly what is rendered.
+
+    A numerical blow-up anywhere (off-hyperboloid sqrt in develop/center,
+    nonfinite coordinates) is a hard rejection (err = inf), never a crash.
+    """
+    from realize_h import mdot, side_length
+    n_unv = 0
+    try:
+        raw, _, events = develop_h(faces, bd, alpha_rad)
+        ell = side_length(alpha_rad)
+        # closure gate (G1 r4): rigid placement can keep edge lengths
+        # near-exact while paths disagree, so every closure event is
+        # gated on the PER-EVENT normalized Lorentz discrepancy between
+        # the predicted and retained lifts -- acosh of the normalized
+        # Minkowski product, a Lorentz-invariant hyperbolic distance,
+        # frame- and scale-independent (the earlier global resid/scale
+        # form was frame-dependent: one large unrelated coordinate
+        # weakened every check). Bound: within 1% of an edge length.
+        for d, D in events:
+            qd, qp = -mdot(D, D), -mdot(raw[d], raw[d])
+            if not (math.isfinite(qd) and math.isfinite(qp)
+                    and qd > 0.0 and qp > 0.0
+                    and float(D[3]) > 0.0 and float(raw[d][3]) > 0.0):
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            q = -mdot(D, raw[d]) / math.sqrt(qd * qp)
+            # for unit timelike lifts q >= 1 exactly; materially below 1
+            # is numerical corruption (G1 r5). Materiality is SCALE-AWARE
+            # with the MEASURED coefficient (closure_C.txt): the Minkowski
+            # products cancel at coordinate scale s with absolute error
+            # <= C_CLOSURE*eps*s^2. The uncertainty never becomes an
+            # allowance (G1 r6): where resolvable, the frame is certified
+            # against the conservative UPPER bound (largest closure
+            # consistent with the measurement). Where the noise floor
+            # ALONE exceeds the budget, closure is unverifiable in
+            # float64: certify-where-resolvable (PD ruling 2026-07-22) --
+            # the event is skipped, counted, and the caller records the
+            # caveat; corruption rejection still applies.
+            s = max(float(np.max(np.abs(D))), float(np.max(np.abs(raw[d]))))
+            tol_q = max(1e-9, C_CLOSURE * 2.22e-16 * s * s)
+            if not math.isfinite(q) or q < 1.0 - tol_q:
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            if math.acosh(1.0 + tol_q) > EDGE_LEN_TOL * ell:
+                n_unv += 1
+                continue
+            dcl_ub = math.acosh(max(1.0, q) + tol_q)
+            if dcl_ub / ell > EDGE_LEN_TOL:
+                return None, dcl_ub / ell, False, n_unv
+        posh = center(raw)
+        norm2 = {}
+        for v, x in posh.items():
+            q = -mdot(x, x)
+            if not (math.isfinite(q) and q > 0.0 and float(x[3]) > 0.0):
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            norm2[v] = q
+        err = 0.0
+        for u, v in (edges or _edges_of(faces)):
+            m = -mdot(posh[u], posh[v]) / math.sqrt(norm2[u] * norm2[v])
+            # same clamp discipline as the closure gate (G1 r5): q >= 1
+            # for unit timelike vectors; materially below 1 = corruption
+            if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 1.0 - 1e-9:
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            dh = math.acosh(m) if m > 1.0 else 0.0
+            err = max(err, abs(dh - ell) / ell)
+        if not math.isfinite(err):
+            return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+    except (ValueError, FloatingPointError, ZeroDivisionError, OverflowError):
+        return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+    return posh, err, err <= EDGE_LEN_TOL, n_unv
+
+
+def glb_movies(name, faces, V, nc, outdir, alphas=None, euclid_end=True,
+               stored=None, write=True):

     grid = bary_grid(SUBDIV)

+def build_net(job, morphs=True):
+    """Backstop wrapper: no single net may crash the whole Pool. Any
+    unexpected exception becomes a BUILD-FAIL result for that net so the
+    rest of the sweep continues (main() reports the FAIL list)."""
+    try:
+        return _build_net(job, morphs)
+    except Exception as e:
+        return (f"v?{job[0]}", f"BUILD-FAIL {type(e).__name__}: {e}")

 def ideal_net_svg(nc, faces):
     """The ideal net: Perron placement via horoz_c (vertex 1 at infinity),
     edges not through vertex 1, rendered as segments."""
     import struct
     try:
         r = subprocess.run([HOROZ], input=(nc + "\n").encode(),
                            capture_output=True, timeout=120)
         data = r.stdout
     except Exception:
         return ''
     V = max(max(f) for f in faces)
     if len(data) < V * 24:
         return ''
     positions = {}
     for vi in range(V):
         u, x, y = struct.unpack_from('<ddd', data, vi * 24)
         if vi == 0 or math.isnan(u):
             continue
         positions[vi + 1] = (x, y)
     edges = set()
     for a, b, c in faces:
         for u2, w2 in ((a, b), (b, c), (a, c)):
             if u2 == 1 or w2 == 1:
                 continue
             edges.add((min(u2, w2), max(u2, w2)))
     return make_svg(positions, sorted(edges))

+def euclid_closure_resid(faces, bd, pos):
+    """Max mismatch between every face's apex as re-placed from its
+    neighbor (walklib.develop's placement formula) and the kept position.
+    Rigid placement makes KEPT edge lengths unit by construction, so bad
+    bends surface only here (proven by the corrupted-store test, G1 r3):
+    walklib.develop skips revisited faces without comparing, computing no
+    residual at all. Checked over ALL directed adjacencies, a superset of
+    any single BFS's closure events. Edge scale is 1, so this bounds the
+    true edge-length error of the displayed configuration."""
+    apex = {}
+    for a, b, c in faces:
+        apex[(a, b)] = c; apex[(b, c)] = a; apex[(c, a)] = b
+    r = 0.0
+    for f0 in faces:
+        for i in range(3):
+            x, y = f0[i], f0[(i + 1) % 3]
+            d = apex[(y, x)]
+            A, B, P = pos[x], pos[y], pos[f0[(i + 2) % 3]]
+            M = 0.5 * (A + B)
+            w = P - M
+            w = w / np.linalg.norm(w)
+            n = np.cross(w, B - A)
+            n = n / np.linalg.norm(n)
+            th = bd[tuple(sorted((x, y)))]
+            D = M + (math.sqrt(3) / 2) * (-math.cos(th) * w + math.sin(th) * n)
+            r = max(r, float(np.max(np.abs(D - pos[d]))))
+    return r
+
+

 def bary_grid(n):
     """barycentric weights + triangle pattern for one subdivided face,
     wound consistently with the face's (a, b, c) orientation."""
     pts, idx = [], {}
     for i in range(n + 1):
         for j in range(n + 1 - i):
             idx[(i, j)] = len(pts)
             pts.append(((n - i - j) / n, i / n, j / n))
     tris = []
     for i in range(n):
         for j in range(n - i):
             a, b, c = idx[(i, j)], idx[(i + 1, j)], idx[(i, j + 1)]
             tris.append((a, b, c))
             if j < n - i - 1:
@@ -241,170 +286,508 @@ def subdivided_frame(faces, P, grid):
         tris += [(base + i, base + j, base + k) for i, j, k in pat]
     return verts, tris
 
 
 def klein_to_poincare(verts):
     out = []
     for x, y, z in verts:
         q = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - (x * x + y * y + z * z)))
         s = 1.0 / (1.0 + q)
         out.append((x * s, y * s, z * s))
     return out
 
 
 def align_frames(frames):
     """remove the spurious rigid rotation between consecutive frames
     (each is developed and centered independently), anchoring at the
     LAST frame: the Euclidean end keeps the flat developer's own
     coordinates -- the same frame the red-black GLB is built in -- and
     each earlier frame is rotated onto its successor (best-fit proper
     rotation via SVD, the standard point-set superposition recipe)."""
     import numpy as np
     n = len(frames)
     out = [None] * n
     out[-1] = np.asarray(frames[-1], float)
     for k in range(n - 2, -1, -1):
         A = np.asarray(frames[k], float)
         A = A - A.mean(axis=0)
         B = out[k + 1] - out[k + 1].mean(axis=0)
         U, _, Vt = np.linalg.svd(A.T @ B)
         d = 1.0 if np.linalg.det(U @ Vt) > 0 else -1.0
         R = U @ np.diag([1.0, 1.0, d]) @ Vt
         out[k] = A @ R
     return [[tuple(v) for v in F] for F in out]

-    then either the exact Euclidean solid (alpha_max = 60) or the

-    alpha_max = 360/maxdeg)."""
+# Length-fidelity gate (PD spec 2026-07-22, final): every edge of a
+# displayed frame must have the length it is supposed to have, within 1%.
+# Hyperbolic frames: every edge of every equilateral face has exact
+# hyperbolic length ell(alpha) (realize_h.side_length); the metric is
+# max over edges of |d_H(u,v) - ell| / ell, with d_H = acosh(-mdot).
+# Euclidean end frame: every edge has length 1; max |len - 1|.
+# Accumulated development divergence lands exactly here: edges whose
+# endpoints arrive via different development paths inherit the drift.
+EDGE_LEN_TOL = 1e-2
+
+
+def _edges_of(faces):
+    return {tuple(sorted((a, b))) for f in faces
+            for a, b in zip(f, f[1:] + f[:1])}
+
+
+# Undented display gate (PD 2026-07-22: "make sure what we display is
+# actually undented"). dent_index link-turning check (port of
+# undented/src/dent_check.c inner loop -- turning per vertex, NEVER
+# volume sign) run on the displayed coordinates themselves. Degenerate
+# case, measured 2026-07-22 on the v12 pancake: an exactly-coplanar
+# vertex link makes every triple product B.(AxC) exact-zero, and where
+# den < 0 the atan2 lands on the branch cut -- +-pi decided by 1e-17
+# rounding noise, so per-vertex totals {-2pi, 0, +2pi} are branch picks,
+# not geometry. Such vertices are excluded: a dent needs non-planarity,
+# and a coplanar link is the flat degenerate boundary (pancakes). Their
+# flatness is already certified by the solved bends (all-plus turning
+# system). Calibration over all 2245 stores
+# (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/dent_turn.tsv): every displayed hero
+# and morph frame passes; tightest genuine margin +3.0e-7; noise floor
+# ~1e-16.
+DENT_TURN_TOL = 1e-9        # reject a displayed frame if min turn < -this
+COPLANAR_NUM_TOL = 1e-12    # all |B.(AxC)| below this => degenerate vertex
+FLAT_BEND_TOL = 1e-6        # bend within this of {0, +-pi} counts as flat/fold
+
+
+def link_rings(faces):
+    """vertex -> cyclic neighbor ring, from the face structure."""
+    succ = {}
+    for a, b, c in faces:
+        succ.setdefault(a, {})[b] = c
+        succ.setdefault(b, {})[c] = a
+        succ.setdefault(c, {})[a] = b
+    rings = {}
+    for v, nxt in succ.items():
+        start = next(iter(nxt))
+        ring, cur = [start], nxt[start]
+        while cur != start:
+            ring.append(cur)
+            cur = nxt[cur]
+        rings[v] = ring
+    return rings
+
+
+def display_min_turn(pos, rings, bends=None):
+    """(min_turn, bad) over the displayed positions. min_turn is the
+    minimum link turning over non-degenerate vertices (+inf if none).
+
+    G1 revision (consult 2026-07-22-161343): a coplanar-degenerate vertex
+    (all |B.(AxC)| < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL, where the atan2 branch is noise)
+    is SKIPPED only when its flatness is independently certified by the
+    solved bends -- every incident bend within FLAT_BEND_TOL of 0 or
+    +-pi, the doubled-flat/pancake signature. A coplanar link WITHOUT
+    that certificate may hide a near-flat dent, so bad=True and the
+    caller must reject the frame. Nonfinite coordinates or turning sums
+    also set bad=True.
+    """
+    mt, bad = float('inf'), False
+    for v, ring in rings.items():
+        k = len(ring)
+        if k < 3:
+            continue
+        dirs = []
+        for nb in ring:
+            d = np.asarray(pos[nb], float) - np.asarray(pos[v], float)
+            n = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
+            if not math.isfinite(n):
+                bad = True
+                break
+            dirs.append(d / (n if n > 1e-15 else 1e-15))
+        else:
+            nums, dens = [], []
+            for i in range(k):
+                A, B, C = dirs[(i - 1) % k], dirs[i], dirs[(i + 1) % k]
+                nums.append(float(np.dot(B, np.cross(A, C))))
+                dens.append(float(np.dot(A, B) * np.dot(B, C) - np.dot(A, C)))
+            if max(abs(x) for x in nums) < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL:
+                flat_ok = bends is not None
+                if flat_ok:
+                    for nb in ring:
+                        b = bends.get(tuple(sorted((v, nb))))
+                        # a MISSING bend is uncertified, never flat (G1 r2)
+                        if b is None or not (abs(b) < FLAT_BEND_TOL
+                                             or abs(abs(b) - math.pi) < FLAT_BEND_TOL):
+                            flat_ok = False
+                            break
+                if not flat_ok:
+                    bad = True       # degenerate link, flatness uncertified
+                continue
+            t = sum(math.atan2(n_, d_) for n_, d_ in zip(nums, dens))
+            if not math.isfinite(t):
+                bad = True
+                continue
+            mt = min(mt, t)
+            continue
+        break                        # nonfinite direction: stop, frame bad
+    return mt, bad
+
+
+# closure-noise coefficient, MEASURED (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/
+# closure_C.txt: 756001 clean-frame events; |q-1|/(eps*s^2) median 0.38,
+# p99 7.6, large-s (>1e5) bound 62). C = 128 = 2x the measured bound.
+C_CLOSURE = 128.0
+
+
+def develop_h_certified(faces, bd, alpha_rad, edges=None):
+    """develop_h -> center -> edge-length gate ON THE CENTERED (displayed)
+    coordinates. Returns (pos_centered, err, ok, n_unverifiable):
+    err = max relative hyperbolic edge-length error vs ell(alpha);
+    n_unverifiable = closure events where the float64 noise floor alone
+    exceeds the 1% budget, so closure is certified-where-resolvable and
+    skipped there (PD ruling 2026-07-22), corruption rejection still
+    applying. Callers record a caveat when n_unverifiable > 0.
+
+    G1 revisions (consult 2026-07-22-161343): the distance is the
+    NORMALIZED form d_H = acosh(-mdot(u,v)/sqrt((-mdot(u,u))(-mdot(v,v))))
+    -- the raw acosh(-mdot) is valid only on exact unit timelike vectors
+    and silently mis-measures off-hyperboloid drift; every point must be
+    finite, timelike (-mdot(x,x) > 0), and future (x4 > 0); certification
+    runs after center() so it validates exactly what is rendered.
+
+    A numerical blow-up anywhere (off-hyperboloid sqrt in develop/center,
+    nonfinite coordinates) is a hard rejection (err = inf), never a crash.
+    """
+    from realize_h import mdot, side_length
+    n_unv = 0
+    try:
+        raw, _, events = develop_h(faces, bd, alpha_rad)
+        ell = side_length(alpha_rad)
+        # closure gate (G1 r4): rigid placement can keep edge lengths
+        # near-exact while paths disagree, so every closure event is
+        # gated on the PER-EVENT normalized Lorentz discrepancy between
+        # the predicted and retained lifts -- acosh of the normalized
+        # Minkowski product, a Lorentz-invariant hyperbolic distance,
+        # frame- and scale-independent (the earlier global resid/scale
+        # form was frame-dependent: one large unrelated coordinate
+        # weakened every check). Bound: within 1% of an edge length.
+        for d, D in events:
+            qd, qp = -mdot(D, D), -mdot(raw[d], raw[d])
+            if not (math.isfinite(qd) and math.isfinite(qp)
+                    and qd > 0.0 and qp > 0.0
+                    and float(D[3]) > 0.0 and float(raw[d][3]) > 0.0):
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            q = -mdot(D, raw[d]) / math.sqrt(qd * qp)
+            # for unit timelike lifts q >= 1 exactly; materially below 1
+            # is numerical corruption (G1 r5). Materiality is SCALE-AWARE
+            # with the MEASURED coefficient (closure_C.txt): the Minkowski
+            # products cancel at coordinate scale s with absolute error
+            # <= C_CLOSURE*eps*s^2. The uncertainty never becomes an
+            # allowance (G1 r6): where resolvable, the frame is certified
+            # against the conservative UPPER bound (largest closure
+            # consistent with the measurement). Where the noise floor
+            # ALONE exceeds the budget, closure is unverifiable in
+            # float64: certify-where-resolvable (PD ruling 2026-07-22) --
+            # the event is skipped, counted, and the caller records the
+            # caveat; corruption rejection still applies.
+            s = max(float(np.max(np.abs(D))), float(np.max(np.abs(raw[d]))))
+            tol_q = max(1e-9, C_CLOSURE * 2.22e-16 * s * s)
+            if not math.isfinite(q) or q < 1.0 - tol_q:
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            if math.acosh(1.0 + tol_q) > EDGE_LEN_TOL * ell:
+                n_unv += 1
+                continue
+            dcl_ub = math.acosh(max(1.0, q) + tol_q)
+            if dcl_ub / ell > EDGE_LEN_TOL:
+                return None, dcl_ub / ell, False, n_unv
+        posh = center(raw)
+        norm2 = {}
+        for v, x in posh.items():
+            q = -mdot(x, x)
+            if not (math.isfinite(q) and q > 0.0 and float(x[3]) > 0.0):
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            norm2[v] = q
+        err = 0.0
+        for u, v in (edges or _edges_of(faces)):
+            m = -mdot(posh[u], posh[v]) / math.sqrt(norm2[u] * norm2[v])
+            # same clamp discipline as the closure gate (G1 r5): q >= 1
+            # for unit timelike vectors; materially below 1 = corruption
+            if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 1.0 - 1e-9:
+                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+            dh = math.acosh(m) if m > 1.0 else 0.0
+            err = max(err, abs(dh - ell) / ell)
+        if not math.isfinite(err):
+            return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+    except (ValueError, FloatingPointError, ZeroDivisionError, OverflowError):
+        return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
+    return posh, err, err <= EDGE_LEN_TOL, n_unv
+
+
+def glb_movies(name, faces, V, nc, outdir, alphas=None, euclid_end=True,
+               stored=None, write=True):
+    """Two animated morph GLBs (Poincare, Klein): ideal end first, then
+    either the exact Euclidean solid (alpha_max = 60) or the hyperbolic
+    realization at the final rung (degree-7 nets: alpha_max = 360/maxdeg).
+
+    Consumer mode: when `stored` (the data/bends record: {"hero": bd,
+    "morph": {rounded-alpha: bd}}) provides a rung, its bends are used and
+    the solver is never invoked for it -- the deterministic no-solver
+    build path. Missing rungs fall back to the solver (producer mode) and
+    are returned in `solved` for persistence.
+
+    Every develop_h frame is certified (develop_h_certified, Klein-length
+    gate). If any frame fails, NO GLB is written and a reason is returned
+    -- never a partial or silently-broken morph. Returns (nframes, note,
+    solved). The euclidean end frame uses the flat developer
+    (walklib.develop), which the v506 diagnosis exonerated, so it is not
+    gated here.
+    """
     grid = bary_grid(SUBDIV)
     fp, fk, tris = [], [], None

+    unv_total = 0                   # closure events unverifiable in float64
+    edges = _edges_of(faces)
+    rings = link_rings(faces)
     for a in (alphas or MORPH_ALPHAS):

-        vk, tris = subdivided_frame(faces, klein(pos), grid)
-        fk.append(vk)
-        fp.append(klein_to_poincare(vk))
+        bd = (stored or {}).get("morph", {}).get(round(a, 6))
+        if bd is None:
+            if stored is not None:
+                # store present => store-only (G1 r2): never fall back to
+                # the solver, neither in certify-only nor in build mode --
+                # an incomplete store means the morphs stay unbuilt.

+            bends = solve_alpha(nc, a)
+            if bends is None:
+                return 0, f"solve_alpha failed at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
+            bd = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends.items()}
+            solved[a] = bd
+        posh, err, ok, nunv = develop_h_certified(faces, bd, math.radians(a), edges)
+        unv_total += nunv
+        if not ok:
+            return 0, f"edge length err {err:.2e} at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total

+            return 0, f"uncertified degenerate frame at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
+        if mt < -DENT_TURN_TOL:
+            return 0, f"dented frame at alpha={a:.2f} (min turn {mt:.2e})", solved, unv_total
+        if write:
+            vk, tris = subdivided_frame(faces, K, grid)
+            fk.append(vk)
+            fp.append(klein_to_poincare(vk))
     if euclid_end:
-        bends60 = solve_prove_60(nc)
-        if bends60 is None:
-            return 0

-        ve, tris = subdivided_frame(faces, {v: p - ctr for v, p in pos60.items()}, grid)
-        fp.append(ve)
-        fk.append(ve)
+        bd60 = (stored or {}).get("hero")
+        if bd60 is None:
+            bends60 = solve_prove_60(nc)
+            if bends60 is None:
+                return 0, "solve_prove_60 failed (euclidean end frame)", solved, unv_total
+            bd60 = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends60.items()}
+        pos60, _ = develop(faces, bd60)
+        cres = euclid_closure_resid(faces, bd60, pos60)
+        eerr = max(abs(float(np.linalg.norm(pos60[u] - pos60[v])) - 1.0)
+                   for u, v in edges)
+        if not (math.isfinite(eerr) and math.isfinite(cres)) \
+                or cres > EDGE_LEN_TOL or eerr > EDGE_LEN_TOL:
+            return 0, f"euclid closure/edge err {cres:.2e}/{eerr:.2e}", solved, unv_total
+        mt, dbad = display_min_turn(pos60, rings, bd60)

     fp = align_frames(fp)
     fk = align_frames(fk)

+    return len(fp), None, solved, unv_total

+def build_net(job, morphs=True):
+    """Backstop wrapper: no single net may crash the whole Pool. Any
+    unexpected exception becomes a BUILD-FAIL result for that net so the
+    rest of the sweep continues (main() reports the FAIL list)."""
+    try:
+        return _build_net(job, morphs)
+    except Exception as e:
+        return (f"v?{job[0]}", f"BUILD-FAIL {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
+
+
 def main(input_path=None):
     """Build records + artifacts + pages for the nets in input_path
     (default data/nets_pages.txt, lines: name netcode). Listing pages,
     galleries, and the front page are special.py's job."""

     # viewer wrappers: source of truth is builders/assets/

         shutil.copy(os.path.join(HERE, "assets", w), os.path.join(OUT, w))
     vsrc = os.path.join(HERE, "assets", "vendor")
     if os.path.isdir(vsrc):
         shutil.copytree(vsrc, os.path.join(OUT, "vendor"), dirs_exist_ok=True)
     jobs = []
     with open(input_path or os.path.join(TOP, "data", "nets_pages.txt")) as f:
         for ln in f:
             t = ln.split()

     e1 = np.array([1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
     e2 = np.array([0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0])
     pos = {a0: O,
            b0: chl * O + shl * e1,
            c0: chl * O + shl * (math.cos(alpha) * e1 + math.sin(alpha) * e2)}
 
     def place(A, B, P, theta):
         M = norm_pt(A + B)
         u = norm_sp(tangent_at(M, B))
         wt = tangent_at(M, P)
         wt = wt - mdot(wt, u) * u
         w = norm_sp(wt)
         # n: spacelike unit vector orthogonal to M, u, w; sign fixed so the
         # Euclidean limit matches the flat developer (measured: Procrustes
         # of the alpha=59.9 frame against walklib.develop needs rotation
         # only, residual ~1e-3; the opposite sign needs a reflection)
         n = None
         for cand in (np.array([0.,0.,1.,0.]), np.array([0.,1.,0.,0.]),
                      np.array([1.,0.,0.,0.]), np.array([0.,0.,0.,1.])):
             t = cand + mdot(cand, M) * M
             t = t - mdot(t, u) * u - mdot(t, w) * w
             q = mdot(t, t)
             if q > 1e-12:
                 n = t / math.sqrt(q); break
         # orientation: det(M, w, u, n) fixes handedness of the frame
         if np.linalg.det(np.stack([M, w, u, n])) > 0:
             n = -n
         d = -math.cos(theta) * w + math.sin(theta) * n
         return cha * M + sha * d
 
     def fkey(f):
         return min(f, f[1:] + f[:1], f[2:] + f[:2])
     seen = {fkey((a0, b0, c0))}
     dq = deque([(a0, b0, c0)])
     resid = 0.0
+    events = []          # closure events: (vertex, second-path placement)
     while dq:

             f1 = (y, x, d)

 def klein(pos):
     return {v: x[:3] / x[3] for v, x in pos.items()}
 
 def poincare(pos):
     return {v: x[:3] / (1.0 + x[3]) for v, x in pos.items()}
 
 def center(pos):
     """boost the Minkowski mean of the vertex 4-vectors to the x4 axis
     (salvaged from the old atlas lorentz.py, matrix form identical)."""
     C = np.mean([x for x in pos.values()], axis=0)
     q = mdot(C, C)
     if q >= 0:
         return dict(pos)
     x1, x2, x3, x4 = C
     c = math.sqrt(-q)
     dd = x4 - x3
     if dd <= 0:
         return dict(pos)
     a = x1 / dd; b = x2 / dd; s = c / dd
     M = np.array([
         [1,     0,     a,                     -a],
         [0,     1,     b,                     -b],
         [-a/s, -b/s,  (1-a*a-b*b+s*s)/(2*s),  (1+a*a+b*b-s*s)/(2*s)],
         [-a/s, -b/s,  (1-a*a-b*b-s*s)/(2*s),  (1+a*a+b*b+s*s)/(2*s)],
     ])
     return {v: M @ x for v, x in pos.items()}
 
 def edge_length_check(faces, pos):
     """max deviation of edge geodesic lengths from their common value."""
     ds = []
     seen = set()
     for a, b, c in faces:
         for x, y in ((a, b), (b, c), (c, a)):
             k = (min(x, y), max(x, y))

   272	def subdivided_frame(faces, P, grid):
   273	    """sample each face on the barycentric grid, linearly in the given
   274	    3d coordinates (in Klein, chords are geodesics, so the linear
   275	    samples lie on the hyperbolic face; in Euclidean they lie on the
   276	    flat face). Returns (verts, tris) with per-face vertex blocks."""
   277	    wts, pat = grid
   278	    verts, tris = [], []
   279	    for a, b, c in faces:
   280	        base = len(verts)
   281	        A, B, C = P[a], P[b], P[c]
   282	        for wa, wb, wc in wts:
   283	            verts.append((wa * A[0] + wb * B[0] + wc * C[0],
   284	                          wa * A[1] + wb * B[1] + wc * C[1],
   285	                          wa * A[2] + wb * B[2] + wc * C[2]))
   286	        tris += [(base + i, base + j, base + k) for i, j, k in pat]
   287	    return verts, tris

   290	def klein_to_poincare(verts):
   291	    out = []
   292	    for x, y, z in verts:
   293	        q = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - (x * x + y * y + z * z)))
   294	        s = 1.0 / (1.0 + q)
   295	        out.append((x * s, y * s, z * s))
   296	    return out

   299	def align_frames(frames):
   300	    """remove the spurious rigid rotation between consecutive frames
   301	    (each is developed and centered independently), anchoring at the
   302	    LAST frame: the Euclidean end keeps the flat developer's own
   303	    coordinates -- the same frame the red-black GLB is built in -- and
   304	    each earlier frame is rotated onto its successor (best-fit proper
   305	    rotation via SVD, the standard point-set superposition recipe)."""
   306	    import numpy as np
   307	    n = len(frames)
   308	    out = [None] * n
   309	    out[-1] = np.asarray(frames[-1], float)
   310	    for k in range(n - 2, -1, -1):
   311	        A = np.asarray(frames[k], float)
   312	        A = A - A.mean(axis=0)
   313	        B = out[k + 1] - out[k + 1].mean(axis=0)
   314	        U, _, Vt = np.linalg.svd(A.T @ B)
   315	        d = 1.0 if np.linalg.det(U @ Vt) > 0 else -1.0
   316	        R = U @ np.diag([1.0, 1.0, d]) @ Vt
   317	        out[k] = A @ R
   318	    return [[tuple(v) for v in F] for F in out]

   321	# Length-fidelity gate (PD spec 2026-07-22, final): every edge of a
   322	# displayed frame must have the length it is supposed to have, within 1%.
   323	# Hyperbolic frames: every edge of every equilateral face has exact
   324	# hyperbolic length ell(alpha) (realize_h.side_length); the metric is
   325	# max over edges of |d_H(u,v) - ell| / ell, with d_H = acosh(-mdot).
   326	# Euclidean end frame: every edge has length 1; max |len - 1|.
   327	# Accumulated development divergence lands exactly here: edges whose
   328	# endpoints arrive via different development paths inherit the drift.
   329	EDGE_LEN_TOL = 1e-2
   330

   332	def _edges_of(faces):
   333	    return {tuple(sorted((a, b))) for f in faces
   334	            for a, b in zip(f, f[1:] + f[:1])}

   337	# Undented display gate (PD 2026-07-22: "make sure what we display is
   338	# actually undented"). dent_index link-turning check (port of
   339	# undented/src/dent_check.c inner loop -- turning per vertex, NEVER
   340	# volume sign) run on the displayed coordinates themselves. Degenerate
   341	# case, measured 2026-07-22 on the v12 pancake: an exactly-coplanar
   342	# vertex link makes every triple product B.(AxC) exact-zero, and where
   343	# den < 0 the atan2 lands on the branch cut -- +-pi decided by 1e-17
   344	# rounding noise, so per-vertex totals {-2pi, 0, +2pi} are branch picks,
   345	# not geometry. Such vertices are excluded: a dent needs non-planarity,
   346	# and a coplanar link is the flat degenerate boundary (pancakes). Their
   347	# flatness is already certified by the solved bends (all-plus turning
   348	# system). Calibration over all 2245 stores
   349	# (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/dent_turn.tsv): every displayed hero
   350	# and morph frame passes; tightest genuine margin +3.0e-7; noise floor
   351	# ~1e-16.
   352	DENT_TURN_TOL = 1e-9        # reject a displayed frame if min turn < -this
   353	COPLANAR_NUM_TOL = 1e-12    # all |B.(AxC)| below this => degenerate vertex
   354	FLAT_BEND_TOL = 1e-6        # bend within this of {0, +-pi} counts as flat/fold

   357	def link_rings(faces):
   358	    """vertex -> cyclic neighbor ring, from the face structure."""
   359	    succ = {}
   360	    for a, b, c in faces:
   361	        succ.setdefault(a, {})[b] = c
   362	        succ.setdefault(b, {})[c] = a
   363	        succ.setdefault(c, {})[a] = b
   364	    rings = {}
   365	    for v, nxt in succ.items():
   366	        start = next(iter(nxt))
   367	        ring, cur = [start], nxt[start]
   368	        while cur != start:
   369	            ring.append(cur)
   370	            cur = nxt[cur]
   371	        rings[v] = ring
   372	    return rings

   375	def display_min_turn(pos, rings, bends=None):
   376	    """(min_turn, bad) over the displayed positions. min_turn is the
   377	    minimum link turning over non-degenerate vertices (+inf if none).
   378	
   379	    G1 revision (consult 2026-07-22-161343): a coplanar-degenerate vertex
   380	    (all |B.(AxC)| < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL, where the atan2 branch is noise)
   381	    is SKIPPED only when its flatness is independently certified by the
   382	    solved bends -- every incident bend within FLAT_BEND_TOL of 0 or
   383	    +-pi, the doubled-flat/pancake signature. A coplanar link WITHOUT
   384	    that certificate may hide a near-flat dent, so bad=True and the
   385	    caller must reject the frame. Nonfinite coordinates or turning sums
   386	    also set bad=True.
   387	    """
   388	    mt, bad = float('inf'), False
   389	    for v, ring in rings.items():
   390	        k = len(ring)
   391	        if k < 3:
   392	            continue
   393	        dirs = []
   394	        for nb in ring:
   395	            d = np.asarray(pos[nb], float) - np.asarray(pos[v], float)
   396	            n = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
   397	            if not math.isfinite(n):
   398	                bad = True
   399	                break
   400	            dirs.append(d / (n if n > 1e-15 else 1e-15))
   401	        else:
   402	            nums, dens = [], []
   403	            for i in range(k):
   404	                A, B, C = dirs[(i - 1) % k], dirs[i], dirs[(i + 1) % k]
   405	                nums.append(float(np.dot(B, np.cross(A, C))))
   406	                dens.append(float(np.dot(A, B) * np.dot(B, C) - np.dot(A, C)))
   407	            if max(abs(x) for x in nums) < COPLANAR_NUM_TOL:
   408	                flat_ok = bends is not None
   409	                if flat_ok:
   410	                    for nb in ring:
   411	                        b = bends.get(tuple(sorted((v, nb))))
   412	                        # a MISSING bend is uncertified, never flat (G1 r2)
   413	                        if b is None or not (abs(b) < FLAT_BEND_TOL
   414	                                             or abs(abs(b) - math.pi) < FLAT_BEND_TOL):
   415	                            flat_ok = False
   416	                            break
   417	                if not flat_ok:
   418	                    bad = True       # degenerate link, flatness uncertified
   419	                continue
   420	            t = sum(math.atan2(n_, d_) for n_, d_ in zip(nums, dens))
   421	            if not math.isfinite(t):
   422	                bad = True
   423	                continue
   424	            mt = min(mt, t)
   425	            continue
   426	        break                        # nonfinite direction: stop, frame bad
   427	    return mt, bad
   428	
   429	
   430	# closure-noise coefficient, MEASURED (notes/gate-calibration-20260722/
   431	# closure_C.txt: 756001 clean-frame events; |q-1|/(eps*s^2) median 0.38,
   432	# p99 7.6, large-s (>1e5) bound 62). C = 128 = 2x the measured bound.
   433	C_CLOSURE = 128.0
   434	
   435	
   436	def develop_h_certified(faces, bd, alpha_rad, edges=None):
   437	    """develop_h -> center -> edge-length gate ON THE CENTERED (displayed)
   438	    coordinates. Returns (pos_centered, err, ok, n_unverifiable):
   439	    err = max relative hyperbolic edge-length error vs ell(alpha);
   440	    n_unverifiable = closure events where the float64 noise floor alone
   441	    exceeds the 1% budget, so closure is certified-where-resolvable and
   442	    skipped there (PD ruling 2026-07-22), corruption rejection still
   443	    applying. Callers record a caveat when n_unverifiable > 0.
   444	
   445	    G1 revisions (consult 2026-07-22-161343): the distance is the
   446	    NORMALIZED form d_H = acosh(-mdot(u,v)/sqrt((-mdot(u,u))(-mdot(v,v))))
   447	    -- the raw acosh(-mdot) is valid only on exact unit timelike vectors
   448	    and silently mis-measures off-hyperboloid drift; every point must be
   449	    finite, timelike (-mdot(x,x) > 0), and future (x4 > 0); certification
   450	    runs after center() so it validates exactly what is rendered.
   451	
   452	    A numerical blow-up anywhere (off-hyperboloid sqrt in develop/center,
   453	    nonfinite coordinates) is a hard rejection (err = inf), never a crash.
   454	    """
   455	    from realize_h import mdot, side_length
   456	    n_unv = 0
   457	    try:
   458	        raw, _, events = develop_h(faces, bd, alpha_rad)
   459	        ell = side_length(alpha_rad)
   460	        # closure gate (G1 r4): rigid placement can keep edge lengths
   461	        # near-exact while paths disagree, so every closure event is
   462	        # gated on the PER-EVENT normalized Lorentz discrepancy between
   463	        # the predicted and retained lifts -- acosh of the normalized
   464	        # Minkowski product, a Lorentz-invariant hyperbolic distance,
   465	        # frame- and scale-independent (the earlier global resid/scale
   466	        # form was frame-dependent: one large unrelated coordinate
   467	        # weakened every check). Bound: within 1% of an edge length.
   468	        for d, D in events:
   469	            qd, qp = -mdot(D, D), -mdot(raw[d], raw[d])
   470	            if not (math.isfinite(qd) and math.isfinite(qp)
   471	                    and qd > 0.0 and qp > 0.0
   472	                    and float(D[3]) > 0.0 and float(raw[d][3]) > 0.0):
   473	                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   474	            q = -mdot(D, raw[d]) / math.sqrt(qd * qp)
   475	            # for unit timelike lifts q >= 1 exactly; materially below 1
   476	            # is numerical corruption (G1 r5). Materiality is SCALE-AWARE
   477	            # with the MEASURED coefficient (closure_C.txt): the Minkowski
   478	            # products cancel at coordinate scale s with absolute error
   479	            # <= C_CLOSURE*eps*s^2. The uncertainty never becomes an
   480	            # allowance (G1 r6): where resolvable, the frame is certified
   481	            # against the conservative UPPER bound (largest closure
   482	            # consistent with the measurement). Where the noise floor
   483	            # ALONE exceeds the budget, closure is unverifiable in
   484	            # float64: certify-where-resolvable (PD ruling 2026-07-22) --
   485	            # the event is skipped, counted, and the caller records the
   486	            # caveat; corruption rejection still applies.
   487	            s = max(float(np.max(np.abs(D))), float(np.max(np.abs(raw[d]))))
   488	            tol_q = max(1e-9, C_CLOSURE * 2.22e-16 * s * s)
   489	            if not math.isfinite(q) or q < 1.0 - tol_q:
   490	                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   491	            if math.acosh(1.0 + tol_q) > EDGE_LEN_TOL * ell:
   492	                n_unv += 1
   493	                continue
   494	            dcl_ub = math.acosh(max(1.0, q) + tol_q)
   495	            if dcl_ub / ell > EDGE_LEN_TOL:
   496	                return None, dcl_ub / ell, False, n_unv
   497	        posh = center(raw)
   498	        norm2 = {}
   499	        for v, x in posh.items():
   500	            q = -mdot(x, x)
   501	            if not (math.isfinite(q) and q > 0.0 and float(x[3]) > 0.0):
   502	                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   503	            norm2[v] = q
   504	        err = 0.0
   505	        for u, v in (edges or _edges_of(faces)):
   506	            m = -mdot(posh[u], posh[v]) / math.sqrt(norm2[u] * norm2[v])
   507	            # same clamp discipline as the closure gate (G1 r5): q >= 1
   508	            # for unit timelike vectors; materially below 1 = corruption
   509	            if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 1.0 - 1e-9:
   510	                return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   511	            dh = math.acosh(m) if m > 1.0 else 0.0
   512	            err = max(err, abs(dh - ell) / ell)
   513	        if not math.isfinite(err):
   514	            return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   515	    except (ValueError, FloatingPointError, ZeroDivisionError, OverflowError):
   516	        return None, float('inf'), False, n_unv
   517	    return posh, err, err <= EDGE_LEN_TOL, n_unv
   518	
   519	
   520	def glb_movies(name, faces, V, nc, outdir, alphas=None, euclid_end=True,
   521	               stored=None, write=True):
   522	    """Two animated morph GLBs (Poincare, Klein): ideal end first, then
   523	    either the exact Euclidean solid (alpha_max = 60) or the hyperbolic
   524	    realization at the final rung (degree-7 nets: alpha_max = 360/maxdeg).
   525	
   526	    Consumer mode: when `stored` (the data/bends record: {"hero": bd,
   527	    "morph": {rounded-alpha: bd}}) provides a rung, its bends are used and
   528	    the solver is never invoked for it -- the deterministic no-solver
   529	    build path. Missing rungs fall back to the solver (producer mode) and
   530	    are returned in `solved` for persistence.
   531	
   532	    Every develop_h frame is certified (develop_h_certified, Klein-length
   533	    gate). If any frame fails, NO GLB is written and a reason is returned
   534	    -- never a partial or silently-broken morph. Returns (nframes, note,
   535	    solved). The euclidean end frame uses the flat developer
   536	    (walklib.develop), which the v506 diagnosis exonerated, so it is not
   537	    gated here.
   538	    """
   539	    grid = bary_grid(SUBDIV)
   540	    fp, fk, tris = [], [], None
   541	    solved = {}                     # alpha -> bend dict, freshly solved only
   542	    unv_total = 0                   # closure events unverifiable in float64
   543	    edges = _edges_of(faces)
   544	    rings = link_rings(faces)
   545	    for a in (alphas or MORPH_ALPHAS):
   546	        bd = (stored or {}).get("morph", {}).get(round(a, 6))
   547	        if bd is None:
   548	            if stored is not None:
   549	                # store present => store-only (G1 r2): never fall back to
   550	                # the solver, neither in certify-only nor in build mode --
   551	                # an incomplete store means the morphs stay unbuilt.
   552	                return 0, f"store incomplete at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
   553	            bends = solve_alpha(nc, a)
   554	            if bends is None:
   555	                return 0, f"solve_alpha failed at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
   556	            bd = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends.items()}
   557	            solved[a] = bd
   558	        posh, err, ok, nunv = develop_h_certified(faces, bd, math.radians(a), edges)
   559	        unv_total += nunv
   560	        if not ok:
   561	            return 0, f"edge length err {err:.2e} at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
   562	        K = klein(posh)              # posh is already centered (displayed)
   563	        mt, dbad = display_min_turn(K, rings, bd)
   564	        if dbad:
   565	            return 0, f"uncertified degenerate frame at alpha={a:.2f}", solved, unv_total
   566	        if mt < -DENT_TURN_TOL:
   567	            return 0, f"dented frame at alpha={a:.2f} (min turn {mt:.2e})", solved, unv_total
   568	        if write:
   569	            vk, tris = subdivided_frame(faces, K, grid)
   570	            fk.append(vk)
   571	            fp.append(klein_to_poincare(vk))
   572	    if euclid_end:
   573	        bd60 = (stored or {}).get("hero")
   574	        if bd60 is None:
   575	            bends60 = solve_prove_60(nc)
   576	            if bends60 is None:
   577	                return 0, "solve_prove_60 failed (euclidean end frame)", solved, unv_total
   578	            bd60 = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends60.items()}
   579	        pos60, _ = develop(faces, bd60)
   580	        cres = euclid_closure_resid(faces, bd60, pos60)
   581	        eerr = max(abs(float(np.linalg.norm(pos60[u] - pos60[v])) - 1.0)
   582	                   for u, v in edges)
   583	        if not (math.isfinite(eerr) and math.isfinite(cres)) \
   584	                or cres > EDGE_LEN_TOL or eerr > EDGE_LEN_TOL:
   585	            return 0, f"euclid closure/edge err {cres:.2e}/{eerr:.2e}", solved, unv_total
   586	        mt, dbad = display_min_turn(pos60, rings, bd60)
   587	        if dbad:
   588	            return 0, "uncertified degenerate euclid frame", solved, unv_total
   589	        if mt < -DENT_TURN_TOL:
   590	            return 0, f"dented euclid frame (min turn {mt:.2e})", solved, unv_total
   591	        if write:
   592	            ctr = sum(pos60.values()) / len(pos60)
   593	            ve, tris = subdivided_frame(faces, {v: p - ctr for v, p in pos60.items()}, grid)
   594	            fp.append(ve)
   595	            fk.append(ve)
   596	    if not write:                    # certification-only pass (reuse path)
   597	        return 0, None, solved, unv_total
   598	    fp = align_frames(fp)
   599	    fk = align_frames(fk)
   600	    write_gltf_morph(os.path.join(outdir, "morph_p.glb"), fp, tris)
   601	    write_gltf_morph(os.path.join(outdir, "morph_k.glb"), fk, tris)
   602	    return len(fp), None, solved, unv_total
   603	
   604	def _build_net(job, morphs=True):
   605	    """One directory per net under nets/, named v{V}{CLERS}: a database
   606	    record (net.json) plus the net's artifacts (rb.glb, clers.glb,
   607	    morph_p.glb, morph_k.glb, ideal_net.svg, clers_layout.svg). The
   608	    page itself is rendered from the record by views.py."""
   609	    import json
   610	    from views import render_page, net_id
   611	    name, nc = job
   612	    faces = [tuple(int(x) for x in f.split(',')) for f in nc.split(';')]
   613	    V = max(max(f) for f in faces)
   614	    vname = f"v{V}{name}"
   615	    nid = net_id(V, name)
   616	    netdir = os.path.join(OUT, "nets", nid)
   617	    os.makedirs(netdir, exist_ok=True)
   618	    # stale morph GLBs never survive a build (G1 r5: deleted FIRST, before
   619	    # store parsing / solving / hero work -- any failure return or raise
   620	    # after this point cannot leave old uncertified morphs advertised)
   621	    for fn in ("morph_p.glb", "morph_k.glb"):
   622	        try:
   623	            os.remove(os.path.join(netdir, fn))
   624	        except FileNotFoundError:
   625	            pass
   626	    deg = {}
   627	    for f in faces:
   628	        for x in f:
   629	            deg[x] = deg.get(x, 0) + 1
   630	    maxdeg = max(deg.values())
   631	    amax = 60.0 if maxdeg <= 6 else 360.0 / maxdeg
   632	    alphas = (MORPH_ALPHAS if maxdeg <= 6 else
   633	              [a * amax / 60.0 for a in MORPH_ALPHAS] + [amax])
   634	    # consumer mode: committed bends (data/bends/<nid>.json) supply every
   635	    # solved quantity; the solver runs only for rungs the store lacks.
   636	    store = None
   637	    spath = os.path.join(TOP, "data", "bends", f"{nid}.json")
   638	    if os.path.exists(spath):
   639	        st = json.load(open(spath))
   640	        store = {"hero": {tuple(int(x) for x in k.split(',')): float(v)
   641	                          for k, v in st["hero"].items()},
   642	                 "morph": {round(m["alpha"], 6):
   643	                           {tuple(int(x) for x in k.split(',')): float(v)
   644	                            for k, v in m["bends"].items()}
   645	                           for m in st["morph"]}}
   646	    # Displayed artifacts are ALWAYS regenerated through the gates from
   647	    # the committed bends (G1 r3): certifying a reconstruction while
   648	    # reusing on-disk GLBs proves nothing about the bytes actually
   649	    # served, and regeneration is deterministic (measured byte-stable),
   650	    # so no reuse optimization is worth that hole. Store present =>
   651	    # store-only; no store => producer mode (solve + persist).
   652	    hero_bends = None      # freshly-solved hero bends (persisted to the store)
   653	    if store:
   654	        bends = store["hero"]
   655	    elif maxdeg <= 6:
   656	        bends = solve_prove_60(nc)
   657	    else:
   658	        bends = solve_alpha(nc, amax)
   659	    if bends is None:
   660	        return (vname, "SOLVE-FAIL")
   661	    if not store:
   662	        hero_bends = bends
   663	    if maxdeg <= 6:
   664	        glb_euclid(name, faces, V, bends, netdir)
   665	    else:
   666	        bd = {tuple(sorted(e)): b for e, b in bends.items()}
   667	        posh, herr, hok, _ = develop_h_certified(faces, bd, math.radians(amax))
   668	        if not hok:                  # deg-7 hero edge gate (G1 r3)
   669	            raise RuntimeError(f"hero fails edge gate ({herr:.2e})")
   670	        K = klein(posh)              # posh already centered (displayed)
   671	        glb_hero(name, faces, V, {v: np.asarray(K[v]) for v in K}, netdir,
   672	                 bends=bd)
   673	    morph_note, morph_bends, n_unv = None, None, 0
   674	    if not morphs:
   675	        built = "skipped (morphs=False)"          # generation-time opt-out
   676	    else:
   677	        nframes, morph_note, morph_bends, n_unv = glb_movies(
   678	            name, faces, V, nc, netdir,
   679	            alphas=alphas, euclid_end=(maxdeg <= 6), stored=store)
   680	        built = (f"omitted ({morph_note})" if morph_note
   681	                 else f"built {nframes} frames")
   682	    inet_path = os.path.join(netdir, "ideal_net.svg")
   683	    if not os.path.exists(inet_path):
   684	        inet = ideal_net_svg(nc, faces)
   685	        if inet:
   686	            with open(inet_path, "w") as f:
   687	                f.write(inet)
   688	    layout_path = os.path.join(netdir, "clers_layout.svg")
   689	    if not os.path.exists(layout_path):
   690	        with open(layout_path, "w") as f:
   691	            f.write(clers_svg(name))
   692	    E = len({tuple(sorted((a, b))) for f in faces for a, b in zip(f, f[1:] + f[:1])})
   693	    have = {k: os.path.exists(os.path.join(netdir, fn)) for k, fn in
   694	            (("rb", "rb.glb"), ("clers_glb", "clers.glb"),
   695	             ("morph_p", "morph_p.glb"), ("morph_k", "morph_k.glb"),
   696	             ("ideal_net", "ideal_net.svg"), ("clers_layout", "clers_layout.svg"))}
   697	    recpath = os.path.join(netdir, "net.json")
   698	    rec = json.load(open(recpath)) if os.path.exists(recpath) else {}
   699	    rec.update({"id": nid, "name": vname, "clers": name, "v": V, "E": E,
   700	                "F": len(faces), "netcode": nc, "artifacts": have,
   701	                "maxdeg": maxdeg})
   702	    if maxdeg > 6:
   703	        rec["alpha_max"] = amax
   704	        rec["morph_labels"] = [f"{a:.1f}" for a in alphas]
   705	    if morph_note:
   706	        rec["morph_note"] = morph_note      # why the morphs are absent (audit)
   707	    else:
   708	        rec.pop("morph_note", None)
   709	    if n_unv and not morph_note:
   710	        # certify-where-resolvable caveat (PD ruling 2026-07-22): closure
   711	        # at the deepest rung(s) sits below the float64 noise floor; the
   712	        # frames are certified by the edge-length + dent gates.
   713	        rec["morph_caveat"] = (f"closure unverifiable on {n_unv} events "
   714	                               f"(float64 noise floor at this scale)")
   715	    else:
   716	        rec.pop("morph_caveat", None)
   717	    rec.setdefault("flags", {})
   718	    rec.setdefault("eisenstein", {"ancestors": [], "descendants": []})
   719	    with open(recpath, "w") as f:
   720	        json.dump(rec, f, indent=1)

   740	            json.dump(store, bf)
   741	    render_page(netdir)
   742	    return (vname, f"OK morphs {built}")
