   744	
   745	def build_net(job, morphs=True):
   746	    """Backstop wrapper: no single net may crash the whole Pool. Any
   747	    unexpected exception becomes a BUILD-FAIL result for that net so the
   748	    rest of the sweep continues (main() reports the FAIL list)."""
   749	    try:
   750	        return _build_net(job, morphs)
   751	    except Exception as e:
   752	        return (f"v?{job[0]}", f"BUILD-FAIL {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
   753	
   754	
   755	def main(input_path=None):
   756	    """Build records + artifacts + pages for the nets in input_path
   757	    (default data/nets_pages.txt, lines: name netcode). Listing pages,
   758	    galleries, and the front page are special.py's job."""
   759	    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
   760	    # viewer wrappers: source of truth is builders/assets/
   761	    import shutil
   762	    for w in ("morph.html", "turntable.html"):
   763	        shutil.copy(os.path.join(HERE, "assets", w), os.path.join(OUT, w))
   764	    vsrc = os.path.join(HERE, "assets", "vendor")
   765	    if os.path.isdir(vsrc):
   766	        shutil.copytree(vsrc, os.path.join(OUT, "vendor"), dirs_exist_ok=True)
   767	    jobs = []
   768	    with open(input_path or os.path.join(TOP, "data", "nets_pages.txt")) as f:
   769	        for ln in f:
   770	            t = ln.split()
   771	            jobs.append((t[0], t[1]))
   772	    # morphs are generated by default; set ATLAS_MORPHS=0 to skip morph
   773	    # generation for this run (a compute choice -- e.g. giant eisenbuddies --
   774	    # NOT a correctness cap: pages render morphs iff the record has them, and
   775	    # morphs enter the record only through the certified gate in glb_movies).
   776	    gen_morphs = os.environ.get("ATLAS_MORPHS", "1") != "0"
   777	    worker = partial(build_net, morphs=gen_morphs)
   778	    with Pool(6) as pool:
   779	        results = pool.map(worker, jobs)
   780	    fails = [n for n, msg in results if "FAIL" in msg]
   781	    omitted = [n for n, msg in results if "omitted" in msg]
   782	    for name, msg in results:
   783	        print(name, msg, flush=True)
   784	    print(f"built {len(results) - len(fails)}/{len(results)}"
   785	          + (f"  morphs omitted (gate): {len(omitted)}" if omitted else "")
   786	          + (f"  FAILURES: {fails}" if fails else ""), flush=True)
   787	    return 1 if fails else 0     # G1: a failed net (incl. dented hero) is a
   788	                                 # failed build -- nonzero exit, never silent
   789	
   790	
   791	if __name__ == "__main__":
   792	    import sys
   793	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None))

         def rank_of(names):
             first = names[0]
             if first in ('tetrahedron', 'cube', 'octahedron', 'icosahedron',
                          'dodecahedron'):
                 return 0
             if not first.startswith(('j', 'pri', 'ant')):
                 return 1
             if first.startswith(('pri', 'ant')):
                 return 2
             return 3

                 c += '&hellip;'
             if hyp:
                 c += ' &middot; hyperbolic, &alpha; &le; 360/7'
             return c

 <h1>Neoplatonic Solids</h1>
 <p class="authors">Peter Doyle, Matthew Ellison</p>
 <p>
 A <em>neoplatonic solid</em> is an undented Euclidean polyhedron
 with equilateral triangle faces, meeting at most six to a vertex.
 <a href="about.html">More &rarr;</a>
 </p>

# Commit evidence — 2026-07-22: display gates, committed bend store, gallery round

Branch `next`. One batch: the display-gate architecture (producer→consumer
bend store + gated regeneration), the gallery reorganization round, and regen
fixes. No Lean; solver untouched (atlas consumes its committed output).

## Staged tree

**builders/personal.py** — (a) Committed bend store: producer persists solved
bends to `data/bends/<nid>.json` — ONLY as complete, gate-passing snapshots;
consumer builds from the store, and with a store present the solver is NEVER
invoked (store-only rule; incomplete store ⇒ morphs stay unbuilt — the
no-giant-chase ruling in code). (b) Always-regenerate: every displayed
artifact is rebuilt from committed bends through the gates each build; no
reuse (regeneration measured byte-deterministic), stale morph GLBs deleted
unconditionally. (c) Length-fidelity gate (PD spec: every displayed edge
within 1% of exact): normalized Lorentz metric post-`center` with per-point
finite/timelike/future validation; per-event normalized Lorentz closure on
every development closure event (≤1% of ell); Euclidean closure over all
directed face adjacencies (`euclid_closure_resid`) + edge lengths; develop
blow-up = hard reject. (d) Undented display gate: `dent_index` link-turning
port on displayed coordinates; coplanar-degenerate vertices skipped only
under the flat-bends certificate (missing bend ⇒ uncertified ⇒ reject).
(e) Hero tripwires raise → BUILD-FAIL; `main()` exits nonzero on any failed
net. (f) Backstop wrapper; `ATLAS_MORPHS=0` opt-out ships nothing;
`morph_note` audit field.

**builders/realize_h.py** — `develop_h` additionally returns closure events
(callers use `[0]`; the gate consumes the third element).

**builders/views.py** — morph block renders only when BOTH morph artifacts
are present.

**builders/special.py** — galleries: classics → Platonic+Archimedean only;
Odds-and-ends off + delinked; Non-prime chip after the three Primes; front
page promises morphs only where certified; dented gallery self-regenerates
from committed `data/walks` via `build.model_glb` (∅-first dent sets, orbit
×k + gap), geometry-exact with published bytes; CSS extended.

**regen_personal.sh** — `set -euo pipefail` (a failed builder step ABORTS —
the personal.py nonzero exit must stop the pipeline, per retro-G2);
numpy/scipy preflight BEFORE the destructive `rm` (wrong interpreter fails
with the site intact); dead network step `fetch_dents.py` removed (consumed
by nothing; must not be an abort vector under -e); `python3.13` + `NEO` →
sibling `projects/neo`; `NEO_SRC`/`PYTHON` overridable. Integration tests
executed: `PYTHON=false` → exit 1 at preflight, site untouched (2245 nets
before and after); `set -euo pipefail; false; echo UNREACHABLE` → exit 1,
unreachable not printed. DISCLOSED: python3.13 lacks scipy on this box, so
the full-regen path (classify.py, its only importer) needs
`PYTHON=/usr/bin/python3` (has numpy 2.0.2 + scipy 1.13.1) or a scipy
install for 3.13 — the consumer build path (personal/symmetry/special)
needs only numpy and runs under python3.13.

**data/bends/** (2245 json, 56 MB) — the committed bend store (2026-07-22
full solver run; deterministic — bend-identical repeat solves, byte-identical
GLB round-trip).

**data/dentings.tsv** — published dent-set scan manifest (47 nets, 195
dentings).

**notes/gate-calibration-20260722/** — the calibration record: `rel_v50.tsv`,
`klein_v50.tsv` (superseded metrics, kept as history), `edge_len.tsv`,
`dent_turn.tsv`, `numcal.txt` (|num| separation ×1.1e5; 4449 skips, 0
uncertified), `closure_C.txt` (measured closure-noise coefficient),
`final_sweep.txt` (zero-state).

**MORPH_GATE_TODO.md** — the full design record: spec, superseded metrics,
all seven G1 rounds with findings and fixes, calibrations, production catches, and the PD certify-where-resolvable ruling.

**notes/codex-consults/** — the 2026-07-21 G2 transcript + seven G1 consults

# Commit evidence — 2026-07-22: display gates, committed bend store, gallery round

Branch `next`. One batch: the display-gate architecture (producer→consumer
bend store + gated regeneration), the gallery reorganization round, and regen
fixes. No Lean; solver untouched (atlas consumes its committed output).

## Staged tree

**builders/personal.py** — (a) Committed bend store: producer persists solved
bends to `data/bends/<nid>.json` — ONLY as complete, gate-passing snapshots;
consumer builds from the store, and with a store present the solver is NEVER
invoked (store-only rule; incomplete store ⇒ morphs stay unbuilt — the
no-giant-chase ruling in code). (b) Always-regenerate: every displayed
artifact is rebuilt from committed bends through the gates each build; no
reuse (regeneration measured byte-deterministic), stale morph GLBs deleted
unconditionally. (c) Length-fidelity gate (PD spec: every displayed edge
within 1% of exact): normalized Lorentz metric post-`center` with per-point
finite/timelike/future validation; per-event normalized Lorentz closure on
every development closure event (≤1% of ell); Euclidean closure over all
directed face adjacencies (`euclid_closure_resid`) + edge lengths; develop
blow-up = hard reject. (d) Undented display gate: `dent_index` link-turning
port on displayed coordinates; coplanar-degenerate vertices skipped only
under the flat-bends certificate (missing bend ⇒ uncertified ⇒ reject).
(e) Hero tripwires raise → BUILD-FAIL; `main()` exits nonzero on any failed
net. (f) Backstop wrapper; `ATLAS_MORPHS=0` opt-out ships nothing;
`morph_note` audit field.

**builders/realize_h.py** — `develop_h` additionally returns closure events
(callers use `[0]`; the gate consumes the third element).

**builders/views.py** — morph block renders only when BOTH morph artifacts
are present.

**builders/special.py** — galleries: classics → Platonic+Archimedean only;
Odds-and-ends off + delinked; Non-prime chip after the three Primes; front
page promises morphs only where certified; dented gallery self-regenerates
from committed `data/walks` via `build.model_glb` (∅-first dent sets, orbit
×k + gap), geometry-exact with published bytes; CSS extended.

**regen_personal.sh** — `set -euo pipefail` (a failed builder step ABORTS —
the personal.py nonzero exit must stop the pipeline, per retro-G2);
numpy/scipy preflight BEFORE the destructive `rm` (wrong interpreter fails
with the site intact); dead network step `fetch_dents.py` removed (consumed
by nothing; must not be an abort vector under -e); `python3.13` + `NEO` →
sibling `projects/neo`; `NEO_SRC`/`PYTHON` overridable. Integration tests
executed: `PYTHON=false` → exit 1 at preflight, site untouched (2245 nets
before and after); `set -euo pipefail; false; echo UNREACHABLE` → exit 1,
unreachable not printed. DISCLOSED: python3.13 lacks scipy on this box, so
the full-regen path (classify.py, its only importer) needs
`PYTHON=/usr/bin/python3` (has numpy 2.0.2 + scipy 1.13.1) or a scipy
install for 3.13 — the consumer build path (personal/symmetry/special)
needs only numpy and runs under python3.13.

**data/bends/** (2245 json, 56 MB) — the committed bend store (2026-07-22
full solver run; deterministic — bend-identical repeat solves, byte-identical
GLB round-trip).

**data/dentings.tsv** — published dent-set scan manifest (47 nets, 195
dentings).

**notes/gate-calibration-20260722/** — the calibration record: `rel_v50.tsv`,
`klein_v50.tsv` (superseded metrics, kept as history), `edge_len.tsv`,
`dent_turn.tsv`, `numcal.txt` (|num| separation ×1.1e5; 4449 skips, 0
uncertified), `closure_C.txt` (measured closure-noise coefficient),
`final_sweep.txt` (zero-state).

**MORPH_GATE_TODO.md** — the full design record: spec, superseded metrics,
all seven G1 rounds with findings and fixes, calibrations, production catches, and the PD certify-where-resolvable ruling.

**notes/codex-consults/** — the 2026-07-21 G2 transcript + seven G1 consults
(161343, 162535, 162856, 163649, 164416, 164845, 175729).

  exit chain).

  recorded RULING is certify-where-resolvable: closure certified
  wherever float64 resolves 1%, unverifiable events skipped + counted +
  disclosed via morph_caveat, corruption rejection retained. (Incident,

  caught by the spot suite, copies diffed identical, excised
  line-precise, all signatures re-verified.)
- r7 175729: APPROVE — "the implementation matches the recorded
  certify-where-resolvable semantics"; C_CLOSURE confirmed against the
  measurement; acceptance hole confirmed removed.

Final zero-state (final_sweep.txt): hero euclid closure max 8.97e-14
